1-10 月进出口数据点评

高技术产业支撑海内外需求   
10 月高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。10 月主要贸易方对我国当月出口同比增
速的正贡献均有收窄。运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口
竞争力。 
 11 月 10 日，海关总署公布数据显示，以美元计价，2026 年 1-10 月，我
国货物贸易出口金额（以下简称： “出口”）同比增长22.7%，增幅（较
1-9 月，下同）走扩 1.4 个百分点；货物贸易进口金额（以下简称：“进
口”）同比增长 29.0%，增幅走扩 1.5 个百分点；当期贸易顺差 10425.9
亿美元。 以人民币计价，1-10 月我国出口同比增长 17.6%， 进口同比增长
23.6%；贸易顺差为 71632.6 亿元。 
单月数据方面，以美元计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 35.2%，增速较 9
月小幅放缓 1.1 个百分点；10 月进口同比增长 41.6%， 增幅较9 月明显走
扩 10.6 个百分点；贸易顺差 1052.8 亿美元。以人民币计价，10 月我国出
口同比增长 28.7%， 增速较9 月收窄 0.9 个百分点； 进口同比增长34.7%，
增速明显加快 10.1 个百分点。从单月环比数据看，10 月我国出口当月环
比下降 7.8%； 进口当月环比下降2.2%。 受到国庆假期的影响，10 月出口、
进口环比有所下滑，但同比增速仍维持较高水平，我国外贸景气度维持
扩张。 
 高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。首先，10 月欧元区、美国 PMI 数据分别
为 52.7%和 55.5%， 均仍处于较高景气度区间；10 月韩国、 越南当月出口
同比增速分别为 93.7%和 53.8%， 反映出当前海外需求的较强韧性， 带动
我国出口表现。其次，高技术制造业需求维持强韧，支撑我国出口同比
增速表现。得益于全球“AI”等高技术制造业投资旺盛，10 月当月我国
部分机电产品出口同比继续较快增长，集成电路出口当月同比增速为
137.1%， 增速较9 月加快 1.1 百分点， 仍维持较明显的正增长， 全球高技
术产业加速发展有望对外需持续形成支撑。 
 此外， 高技术制造业或也对我国进口表现有所支撑；9 月高技术产业工业
增加值同比增长 15.3%，维持较高水平增速；10 月高技术制造业 PMI 指
数 51.3%， 仍维持在扩张区间； 国内高技术产业增长带动相关产品进口增
速表现，10 月我国集成电路进口当月同比增速为 78.3%，增速较 9 月加
快 3.6 个百分点。 
 运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力。 出口方
面，首先，1-10 月集成电路及自动数据处理设备出口金额同比实现较快
增速 ， 同比增速分别为111.1%和 60.6%， 海外高技术产品需求仍较旺盛。 
其次，当期机械设备出口表现较好，通用设备、船舶及汽车（和底盘）
出口同比增速分别为 99.5%、27.7%和 52.2 %；值得注意的是，汽车相关
产品出口额在近年出口较高基数的基础上，维持较快增速，我国汽车行
业出口优势仍较明显。 
此外，劳动密集型产品出口仍相对弱势， 1-10 月多数轻工业产品出口金
额同比增速低于当期出口同比增速整体水平，鞋靴、灯具及家用陶瓷器
皿出口同比负增长；我们认为，运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密
集型产品出口竞争力。 
 进口方面，1-10 月，我国集成电路和自动数据处理设备及其零部件同比
分别增长 65.1%和 136.7%。 
 风险提示： 海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性； 政策效果不
及预期。

### Page 2

2026 年 11 月 10 日 1-10 月进出口数据点评 2

图表 1.进出口数据表现

资料来源：Wind，中银证券

11 月 10 日，海关总署公布数据显示，以美元计价，2026 年 1-10 月，我国货物贸易出口金额（以下
简称：“出口”）同比增长 22.7%，增幅（较 1-9 月，下同）走扩 1.4 个百分点；货物贸易进口金额
（以下简称：“进口”）同比增长 29.0%，增幅走扩 1.5 个百分点；当期贸易顺差 10425.9 亿美元。
以人民币计价，1-10 月我国出口同比增长 17.6%，进口同比增长 23.6%；贸易顺差为 71632.6 亿元。 
单月数据方面，以美元计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 35.2%，增速较 9 月小幅放缓 1.1 个百分点；
10 月进口同比增长 41.6%，增幅较 9 月明显走扩 10.6 个百分点；贸易顺差 1052.8 亿美元。以人民币
计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 28.7%，增速较 9 月收窄 0.9 个百分点；进口同比增长 34.7%，增速明
显加快 10.1 个百分点。从单月环比数据看，10 月我国出口当月环比下降 7.8%；进口当月环比下降
2.2%。受到国庆假期的影响，10 月出口、进口环比有所下滑，但同比增速仍维持较高水平，我国外
贸景气度维持扩张。 
高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。首先，10 月欧元区、美国 PMI 数据分别为 52.7%和 55.5%，均仍处
于较高景气度区间；10 月韩国、越南当月出口同比增速分别为 93.7%和 53.8%，反映出当前海外需
求的较强韧性，带动我国出口表现。其次，高技术制造业需求维持强韧， 支撑我国出口同比增速表
现。得益于全球“AI”等高技术制造业投资旺盛，10 月当月我国部分机电产品出口同比继续较快增
长，集成电路出口当月同比增速为 137.1%，增速较 9 月加快 1.1 百分点，仍维持较明显的正增长，
全球高技术产业加速发展有望对外需持续形成支撑。 
此外，高技术制造业或也对我国进口表现有所支撑；9 月高技术产业工业增加值同比增长 15.3%，维
持较高水平增速；10 月高技术制造业 PMI 指数 51.3%，仍维持在扩张区间；国内高技术产业增长带
动相关产品进口增速表现，10 月我国集成电路进口当月同比增速为 78.3%，增速较 9 月加快 3.6 个
百分点。 
主要贸易方对我国当月出口同比增速的正贡献均有收窄。分国别看，1-10 月我国对美国、欧盟、东
盟 、 日本及非洲等贸易伙伴的出口同比增速均为正 ， 其中对东盟和非洲的出口同比增速分别为 30.5%
和 24.0%，“一带一路”国家对我国外需的支撑仍较明显；此外，同期对美出口同比增速为12.1%，
增幅较 1-9 月走扩 2.3 个百分点。 
从单月数据表现看 ，10 月东盟进出口总额实现 1240.8 亿美元 ， 占比17.2%； 其中 ， 出口同比增长 46.3%，
进口同比增长 42.0%；10 月我国对欧盟进出口总额 758.0 亿美元，占比 10.5%；其中，出口同比增长
24.3%， 进口同比下滑 4.2%；对美国进出口总额 601.2 亿美元 ， 占比8.4%， 其中 ， 出口同比增长 32.9%，
进口同比增长 35.0%。从贡献角度看，10 月东盟、欧盟及美国对当期出口同比增速的正贡献分别为
8.1、3.5 和 3.8 个百分点；10 月以上主要贸易方对我国出口同比增速的正贡献均有缩窄，东盟、欧
盟及美国正贡献分别收窄 0.4、0.2 和 0.4 个百分点。

### Page 3

2026 年 11 月 10 日 1-10 月进出口数据点评 3

图表 2. 1-10 月主要产品的出口同比增速  图表 3. 1-10 月主要产品的进口同比增速

资料来源：Wind，中银证券  资料来源：Wind，中银证券

运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力 。出口方面，首先，1-10 月集成电路及
自动数据处理设备出口金额同比实现较快增速，同比增速分别为 111.1%和 60.6%，海外高技术产品
需求仍较旺盛。 
其次，当期机械设备出口表现较好，通用设备、船舶及汽车（和底盘） 出口同比增速分别为99.5%、
27.7%和 52.2 %； 值得注意的是 ，汽车相关产品出口额在近年出口较高基数的基础上 ，维持较快增速，
我国汽车行业出口优势仍较明显。 
此外，劳动密集型产品出口仍相对弱势，1-10 月多数轻工业产品出口金额同比增速低于当期出口同
比增速整体水平，鞋靴、灯具及家用陶瓷器皿出口同比负增长；我们认为，运价等成本升高或继续
影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力。 
进口方面 ， 1-10 月 ， 我国集成电路和自动数据处理设备及其零部件同比分别增长65.1%和 136.7%。

图表 4. 1-10 月主要出口产品价格同比增速表现   图表 5. 1-10 月主要进口产品价格同比增速表现

资料来源：Wind，中银证券  资料来源：Wind，中银证券

价格因素对 10 月出口同比增速的影响仍较明显。1-10 月我国高技术产品进、出口相对活跃，集成电
路进出口均维持较高的累计同比增速；而从价格表现来看，1-10 月集成电路进口、出口价格同比增
速分别为 58.6%和 107.9%，得益于海外、国内高技术产业投资持续发力，我国部分高技术产品进、
出口量价齐升。 
原材料方面，当前中东地区地缘政治冲突仍有反复，对国内上游产品进口需求有所影响。 据我们测
算，1-10 月原油 、成品油进口单价同比增速均高于 15.0%， 但两项产品进口数量同比降幅均高于 15.0%；
此外，同期铜矿砂（及其精矿）和未锻造的铜（及铜材） 的进口单价同比分别增长32.2%和 36.6%，
但以上两种产品同期进口数量也同比下滑，国内工业特别是制造业上游采购需求或仍受到影响。 
风险提示：海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性；政策效果不及预期。

披露声明 
本报告准确表述了证券分析师的个人观点。该证券分析师声明，本人未在公司内、外部机构兼任有损本人独立性与客
观性的其他职务，没有担任本报告评论的上市公司的董事、监事或高级管理人员；也不拥有与该上市公司有关的任何
财务权益；本报告评论的上市公司或其它第三方都没有或没有承诺向本人提供与本报告有关的任何补偿或其它利益。  
中银国际证券股份有限公司同时声明，将通过公司网站披露本公司授权公众媒体及其他机构刊载或者转发证券研究报
告有关情况。 如有投资者于未经授权的公众媒体看到或从其他机构获得本研究报告的， 请慎重使用所获得的研究报告，
以防止被误导，中银国际证券股份有限公司不对其报告理解和使用承担任何责任。  
评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

Document 011: 10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
美东时间 11 月 6 日，美国劳工部统计局（BLS）发布 10 月非农就业报告。新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显
著高于市场预期的 6.5万人；失业率下行至 4.1%，低于市场预期的 4.2%。
评论：
图1：非农数据总览
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
美国 10 月非农大幅超预期。美国 10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，高于预期的 6.5 万人，8-9 月合计上修
8.4万人，显示此前招聘放缓的程度被高估。商务服务、私人教育医疗和运输仓储贡献约九成净新增就业，
咨询、临时用工、医疗及快递招聘构成主要支撑，部分消费服务和地产相关行业仍然承压。我们认为，
美国就业“喜忧参半”，喜的是美国总体就业市场十分强劲，而忧的是就业质量仍存在瑕疵。失业率降至
4.1%，劳动参与率升至 61.9%，就业增长快于劳动力扩张，且裁员压力有限，说明用工需求仍有韧性。不
过，非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率 U6升至 7.9%，表明部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，数据成色有瑕疵。
数据公布后，12 月加息预期升温，但资产表现分化，市场未全面交易持续收紧。站在当前时点，市场对

就业数据的反应存在两难：过弱容易引发衰退交易，过强又会增加激进加息的担忧。本月数据明显偏强，
2026年11月08日

### Page 2

缓解了增长担忧，也使政策收紧重新成为市场关注点。不过，从盘中表现看，其影响主要集中于强化 12
月加息预期（12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%），尚未体现为对明年更大幅度收紧的全面定价。美
债收益率冲高后，长端回吐涨幅，短端仍高于公布前水平；美元走强、黄金下跌，美股开盘后反弹但随
后有所回落。就业韧性缓解了增长担忧，同时增加了利率对资产估值的约束。
这份非农是否属于沃什在 10 月会议上所说的“需要等待的更多信息”？我们认为，确实如此。就业回暖
印证了经济韧性，减轻了进一步收紧的就业顾虑，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。但它是否足以改变政策路
径，成为决定后续加息幅度的关键变量？我们认为，尚不足以。一是非农数据修订较大，单月改善仍需
多项指标相互印证，或连续数月形成趋势；二是在就业保持韧性、价格压力尚未消退的背景下，通胀仍
是政策权衡的重点，就业改善提供了加息空间，却不能单独决定加息幅度；三是对市场而言，民主党掌
控国会后的财政政策变化、AI 投资及回报前景，会更直接地影响中期增长和利率预期，其重要性超过单

月宏观数据。我们维持 12 月加息 25个基点、明年再加息一次的基准判断。
 新增非农：远超预期，招聘改善仍有结构分化
10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显著高于 6.5 万人的市场预期，且 8-9 月合计上修 8.4 万人，显示招聘
修复强于此前判断。我们认为，企业经营活动扩张与用工需求回补是招聘改善的重要支撑，制造业和服
务业 ISM就业指数均处于扩张区间，也提供了旁证。此外，年末旺季招聘为就业提供有力支撑。
从结构看，医疗、商务服务和物流主导本月就业增长，但行业改善仍不均衡。10 月私营部门新增就业
15.9 万人，其中服务业增加 14.4 万人，商品生产增加 1.5万人；政府部门增加 0.6万人。专业商业服务
增加 7.0万人，主要由咨询和临时用工带动；私人教育医疗增加 5.3万人，其中医疗保健增加 4.16 万人，
医院及居家医疗是主要来源；运输仓储增加 2.48 万人，主要由快递招聘支撑，仓储就业则略有减少。上
述三个行业合计贡献约九成净新增就业，而休闲酒店、批发和建筑分别减少 1.3万、0.91万和 0.3万人，

表明招聘修复尚未覆盖全部消费服务及地产相关环节。

图4：分行业新增非农就业 图5：本月新增就业人数较上月变化
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
 失业率：就业吸纳改善，广义失业率逆势上升
10 月就业吸纳改善，失业率小幅下降。失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，劳动参与率同步升至 61.9%，说明更多
人进入求职市场后，就业增长仍能消化新增供给。从绝对就业人数上看，就业增长快于劳动力增长。10
月劳动力增加 42.1万人，就业增加 46.3万人，失业人数减少 4.1 万人，就业人口比升至 59.3%。我们认
为，失业率回落伴随着参与率和就业人口比同步上升，反映新增劳动力得到更充分吸收。
不过，广义失业率 U6 由 7.6%升至 7.9%，表明岗位数量改善的同时，部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，就业
修复的质量弱于总量表现。失业者分项中，职位流失者及临时工作结束者合计减少 3.4 万人，其中临时
裁员人数减少 7.9 万人、永久职位流失者减少 1.7 万人，临时工作结束者增加 6.2 万人。再进入者增加

0.8万人、新进入者减少 4.0万人，可见新增求职者积压有所缓解。
我们认为，从 10月非农数据来看，美国就业喜忧参半。喜的是新增就业回升、失业率下降，且裁员压力
有限，用工需求仍有韧性；忧的是非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率上升，就业质量改善滞后于数量增长。
往后看，我们更关注国会易手后的预算拨款和政府采购。若两党分歧拖慢项目审批与资金到位，依赖公
共项目的工程建设、科研及专业服务企业将承受订单兑现压力，招聘与就业也将承压。
图6：美国失业率 图7：美国劳动参与率与新增登记失业人数

图8：不同人群失业率 图9：非农平均时薪增速

 往后看：近期加息预期强化，中期路径仍取决于通胀
非农数据公布后，市场上调 12 月加息预期。12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%，较前一日提高约 10
个百分点，表明就业回暖增强了市场对联储继续收紧的判断。2027 年仍定价加息一次，市场对本轮加息
的终点仍未形成一致预期。资产价格先交易加息预期升温，随后出现分化，强非农尚未推动远期利率全
面上行。数据发布后 1 小时，2 年期美债收益率上行约 2.9个基点，10 年和 30 年期收益率则在冲高后分
别回落约 0.4 和 2.3 个基点；美元小幅走强，黄金下跌约 34 美元。纳指和标普 500 开盘后一度较开盘上
涨约 0.7%和 0.8%，随后回吐部分涨幅。
我们认为，此次非农对市场的直接影响有限，但确实是沃什等待的重要数据之一。就业回暖进一步印证
经济韧性，使联储能够更多关注通胀，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。不过，油价回落并不意味着通胀压力

已经消退，核心商品和服务价格仍有黏性。对市场而言，国会易手后的财政政策走向、AI 投资能否持续，
是比单月非农更重要的中期主线。这些变化会先影响市场对未来增长和利率的判断，再逐步体现在就业
等宏观数据中。
我们延续先前判断，预计 12 月加息 25 个基点，明年加息一次。我们判断，油价回落将逐步缓解整体通
胀压力，但核心商品成本传导尚未结束，服务价格也仍有黏性，即便能源降温难以成为美联储立即停止
加息的充分理由。当前就业韧性减轻了联储对进一步收紧冲击经济的顾虑，使其能够更多关注价格稳定。
我们暂以 12 月和明年各加息一次为基准，是否进一步收紧，仍取决于价格压力消退的程度。
图10：CME利率期货数据

15席(未扣加州、猶他對D有利部分)
• 實際結果：至美東23:56，D翻轉20席、R翻轉0席；德州D已確定11席，高於新

地圖設計的約8席
• 失靈原因：新選區把D選民打散至拉美裔占多數的「安全R區」，但拉美裔回流
使這些選區由安全轉為競爭，形成反效果
• 象徵：共和黨眾院競選委員會主席Hudson於北卡第9選區落選

8
• 立場比較：稽核後放行vs.立法前暫停
項目 Abbott（R，卸任） Hinojosa（D，當選）
過去立場 曾稱德州為「AI發展的中心」，積極招商 ；6月起轉向管制 2017年州眾議員任內，放寬資料中心銷售稅豁免 就業認定的HB 4038於眾院以144:0通過
暫停方式 8月令PUC、ERCOT稽核併網佇列；9/21 令TCEQ停發資料中心許可至稽核完成 就任後暫停新資料中心許可，至州議會完成立法為 止(有期限、非永久)
成本與補貼 主張廢除銷售稅豁免與其他優惠；大型業 者每年申報用電用水 停止補貼、「讓資料中心自己付錢」；業者須證明 住戶電費水費不因電網升級轉嫁
社區與勞工 主張禁止在鄉村社區興建 居民決定「是否及如何」興建；禁NDA；工會勞 工；威脅供水的用水合約無效

禁止AI或拆除既有案

10
• 藍潮對股債的影響
資產 亞盤即時反應 短期 中期
美股大盤 S&P期貨 -0.6% 政策不確定性、主要反映評價面收縮 回歸企業獲利：2026–27年EPS雙位數成長
AI／AIDC 新興雲 -2%～-5% 州級審批、稅優退場與國會調查壓抑評價 大型CSP資本支出續增，分化加劇，體質佳者有望勝出
政策受惠股 健保、太陽能 +2%～ +5% ACA補貼、Medicaid刪減延後題材 需視撥款與債限談判落實
美債長端 10Y利率 -2bp 新增赤字立法受阻，赤字大幅擴張風險下 降 AI發債放緩為長端主要利多

### Page 11

【產業看法-受惠】
新政治格局下，醫療服務、綠能儲能與平價消費相對受惠

14
產業 受惠邏輯 代表個股
醫院 恢復ACA補貼提升投保人數、降低無償照護；Medicaid刪減延後 HCA、THC、UHS、CYH
ACA／Medicaid
管理式醫療 MS熱力圖於D橫掃情境評為正向；2028單一支付制為尾部風險 CNC、OSCR、MOH
綠能／儲能
公用事業 D推動綠能稅優作為談判籌碼；資料中心電價壓力轉向「誰付費」 FSLR、NEE、ENPH、BEP
電網建設 AI仍需電網，爭議在誰付費而非是否興建 PWR、HUBB、MTZ、ITRI
平價消費 SNAP與Medicaid刪減延後支撐低收入戶 WMT、DG、COST
住宅建商 可負擔住房為百日議程 DHI、LEN、PHM、TOL
政府服務 非國防支出與醫療支出受惠 MMS、ICFI、TYL
配置重點：醫療服務為藍潮最直接受惠，近期市場提前反應主要由估值驅動，明年或有望看見獲利改善推動股價上漲，可逢回布局當衛星部位；綠能受惠

於政策但利率敏感，偏好具儲能與大型負載合約的公用事業（低電價通膨州、工商業用電占比高）；消費端以平價、必需消費優於關稅敏感的非必需消費

2027年CSP建設續推進，留意2028年新增算力放緩與選舉議題
資料來源：各公司財報、POWER Magazine、BofA、台新投顧整理，資料時間：2026/11
15
• 大型CSP AIDC加速上線（年增GW）
成長放緩主要來自電力與審批瓶頸，而非需求消失；新興雲擴張最仰賴新建案，受
新規影響最直接
• 建設時程與風險判斷
時點 政策進程 對CSP建設的影響
4Q26 跛腳鴨；州長當選人1月就任 在建案照常；部分新案觀望
1H27 各州議會會期，幾乎所有州將討論資 料中心法規；暫停令平均約346天 影響有限：2027年專案多已取得 用地、電力與許可
2H27 大型負載費率、成本分攤與資格審查 規則定案；過半地方暫停令到期 規則明朗後審批恢復，但成本上 升
2028 總統大選年；稅優縮減提案、規劃中 專案須按新規送審 新案上線時程延後，2028年容量 增速恐下修
2029+ 若D入主白宮，聯邦稅優與審查立法機 率上升 聯邦層級尾部風險
• 2027年支撐：Meta 2026資本支出指引1,350–1,450億美元、Alphabet 1,950–2,050億美元且2027年「顯著增加」、Amazon約2,200億美元；雲端積壓訂單約1.8兆美

元。2028年觀察：規劃中(未核准)專案的審批通過率、稅優存廢與新興雲融資成本
• AIDC產業看法：暫停主張多針對「尚未核准」專案，已營運與在建案多不受影響；但審查與稅優退場會拉長新案時程、墊高資金成本，對體質差、據點集中、仰賴外部
融資的新興雲與中小雲服務商衝擊最大；據點分散、自有現金流的大型CSP與資料中心REITs相對受惠於供給受限

長為主要驅動因素

AIDC建設受限，資金或由AI硬體供應鏈轉向相關軟體股

資料來源：Reuters、Wind、美國國會、美國財政部、台新投顧整理，資料時間：2026/11
【中美關係】
法案 黨派 影響產業 進度
國會貿易權改革法案/撤銷特定關稅法案 民主黨 輸美消費品、電子產品 兩案均已提案
半導體設備管制協調法案(MATCH) 跨黨派 半導體設備、晶圓代工 委員會已通過
晶片設備採購限制法案(Chip EQUIP) 跨黨派 半導體設備 委員會已通過
先進 AI 晶片出口監督法案
(AI OVERWATCH) 跨黨派 AI 晶片、運算能力供應 鏈 委員會已通過
生物安全法案(BIOSECURE) 跨黨派 生技研發與製造服務 已成法
對外投資國安法案(COINS) 跨黨派 半導體、AI 等敏感科技 已成法
• 國會對中貿易、科技與投資重點法案 • 中國科技與生技龍頭海外營收占比
• 晶片出口許可可能面臨更嚴格的審查：新國會就任後，民主黨可透過聽證與調查，要求商務部說明對中晶片出口許可的核發依據。
若商務部加強終端用途核驗，企業可能先面臨審批拉長、交付延後。

• 科技管制有跨黨支持，新增限制仍有立法門檻：先進晶片與半導體設備管制已有跨黨派支持，新增限制仍須兩院通過相同文本，並
面對總統可能否決。BIOSECURE 與 COINS 已成法，後續分別看聯邦採購限制涵蓋哪些供應商，以及對外投資規則涵蓋哪些交易。
• 強迫勞動查核可能成為國會監督重點：民主黨可能加強監督《維吾爾強迫勞動預防法》（UFLPA）的執行情況。若美國海關加嚴查
核，紡織與太陽能產品的輸美供應鏈可能面臨更多原料溯源舉證要求，通關時間也可能拉長。

第一部分 中国制造业和非制造业 PMI数据表格回顾】

数据来源：银河期货、wind资讯

第二部分  内外需劈叉继续修正 制造业环比走势趋弱

2026年 10月制造业 PMI和大部分分项均下降，制造业 PMI达到 49.3，时隔 1个月再度
回到 50荣枯线以下，预期 50，但符合季节性走势。其中主要原材料购进价格分项下降 4.0，
达到 56.8；出厂价格分项下降 2.3，达到51.7；采购量分项下降 2.1，达到48.9；进口分项
下降 2.2，达到 47.0；在手订单分项下降 1.9，达到 44.1；生产分项下跌 1.9，达到 49.8；
新订单分项下降 1.1，达到49.4，上述是10月制造业 PMI主要的下降分项。原材料库存分项

### Page 2

中国 PMI观察报告 2026年 11月 9日 
宏观数据报告 
第 2 页 共 7 页 
上升 0.2，达到 48.4；内需上升 0.2；供应商时间上升 0.1，达到 50.2；上述三个分项是 10
月制造业 PMI全部上升的分项。整个10月制造业 PMI中，除出厂价格、原材料购进价格、配
送时间和生产活动预期外，绝大部分分项处于50融枯线以下，显示经济处于季节性趋弱的状
态中，但从内外需环比走势看，10月内需继续回升，外需有所下降，经济结构继续改善。 
季节性看，2026年 10月制造业 PMI各分项走势基本符合季节性回落的特征。其中内需
处于历史同期次低；原材料库存处于历史同期最高；新出口订单处于历史同期次高。总结看，
10月制造业PMI表现出宏观经济处于供需双弱、累库存、预期回升的状态，这显示出经济经
历了 8月、9月经济恢复后，10月重回平稳状态，11月和 12月或重回环比回升状态。 
各类企业和不同行业 10月 PMI下滑。各类企业中，大型企、中型和小型企业 PMI均下降，
其中大型企业仍处高位，且处于 50荣枯线以上，中型和小型企业处于 50荣枯线以下。各行
业中，消费品行业、基础材料行业、高技术制造业和装备制造业 PMI均下降。其中高技术制
造业和装备制造业 PMI处于 50荣枯线以上，消费品行业和基础材料行业 PMI处于 50荣枯线
以下，且消费品行业 PMI处于历史同期最低 水平。 
美伊冲突仍是全球经济最大的不确定性，国内商品需求下滑。中国制造业 PMI及进口分
项走势预示中国进出口增速有支撑；工业企业利润增速和 PPI走势正经历拐点。内需低迷的
情况下，国内经济走势将更多依赖政策支撑。预计大宗商品需求内外分化的格局未来将改变。

第三部分  服务业再回低位

2026年 10月非制造业 PMI绝大部分分项下降，时隔 1个月后再度回到 50荣枯线以下。
各分项中，从业人员上升 0.1，达到 46是唯一上升的分项。建筑业分项下降 2.3，达到 48；
新订单分项下降 2.1，达到 44.4；新出口订单分项下降 2，达到 46.5；存货分项下降 1.6，
达到 44.7，上述是下降比较大的分项。 
整体看，10月非制造业 PMI在连续修复后，再回平稳状态，商务活动分项和建筑业分项
再回季节性低位，内需尚可，显示经济恢复之路仍很漫长，但如果地产对经济拖累减弱，确
实是可喜变化。

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

瑞昱 (2379 TT) 
財報與展望均優於預期

永豐觀點 
目前股價持穩於前季法說時點，而公司在財報與展望(包括毛利率、營業費用控管)
似乎均有好轉現象，尤其毛利率似乎出現谷底回溫 (優於研究部原預期的內外承
壓)，又公司在網通/車載等業務持續進展，股價應有上升空間。 
 投資評價與建議 
中立調升至買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控
管，故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66
元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶片
大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力 (或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

ESG 評析 
瑞昱企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

瑞昱為網通晶片大廠 ：瑞昱為全球網通晶片大廠，若以公司營收排名為全球第八大
IC 設計廠商。瑞昱產品線含括 Ethernet、WiFi、藍芽、Switch 與 TV SoC 。網通
領域的參與廠商為博通、Marvell、高通與聯發科等，TV SoC 的參與廠商則包括聯
發科、聯詠等。 
3Q26 獲利優於預期：瑞昱 3Q26 營收 377.32 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+27.9% YoY)，
毛利率 47.4%，稅後獲利 41.26 億元(+8%QoQ，+20%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8 元，獲
利優於預期，主要是營收、毛利率與營業費用控管均較預期佳。瑞昱3Q26 財報細
項要點如下：(1) 營收季增 0.5%，優於研究部原預期的季減 3%，主要是網通業務
成長驅使，以及部分產品價格調漲，從而抵銷筆電/TV/遊戲機需求減緩的衝擊。若
究終端應用分拆，PC 因終端市場需求疲軟，瑞昱 3Q26 PC 比重由前季的 33%降
至 30%(3Q26 PC: 非 PC 比重為 30%:70%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47%微幅增至
47.4%(優於研究部原預期將受製造成本上升而使毛利率承壓)，主要是產品結構變
化(高階產品比重增加 )，以及產品因應成本上揚而進行轉嫁。 (3) 營業費用季減
0.6%，反映公司在投資研發的同時，仍對營業費用謹慎控管。(4) 業外收益僅 6.06
億元(季減 3900 萬元)， 主要是包含金融資產評價損失 。(5) 瑞昱庫存天數雖由2Q26
的 107 天增至 3Q26 的 136 天，不過主要是供應鏈持續有不確定性，公司為確保
客戶供貨的連貫性，故多建置庫存因應。 
4Q26 營收降幅不會如同過往第四季顯著：瑞昱認為(1) 4Q26 營運審慎穩健，雖
第四季通常為營運淡季， 但隨產品結構往高階挪移， 又價格為反映製造成本上升而
仍有調漲事宜，故營收降幅較為平緩，其中PC 需求仍相對疲弱。(3) 2H26 毛利率
將逐步回溫。整體而言，研究處預估瑞昱 4Q26 營收 348.5 億元(-7.6%QoQ，
+32.6%YoY)，毛利率隨產品結構變化與轉嫁而略增至 47.6%，稅後獲利 35.34 億
元(-14%QoQ，+33%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.86 元。

### Page 3

3

2027 年營運應仍能維持成長力道：研究部認為台系 IC 設計族群今明年將持續遭
遇內外交雜的挑戰(內有製造成本攀升、外有消費電子需求疲軟)，不過瑞昱身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品優化與議價能力均相對同業更具競爭力，使得營運表現
應能優於產業狀態(其實從瑞昱財報亦能得證，公司即使 30%營收與 PC 相關，但
其 3Q26 獲利數字與 4Q26 展望均仍穩健)。研究部認為瑞昱動能來自下述因子驅
動：(1) 網通隨電信業者對 25GPON 架構進行結構性測試、乙太網路交換器受益
於硬體升級、消費者與中小企業路由器往Ｍesh/三頻配置/WiFi7 升級，並且企業
級網路管理需求與管理型交換器持續普及中，均將推升瑞昱網通業務長期穩健成
長。(2) WiFi7 滲透率提升。隨瑞昱客戶的新開案均基於 WiFi7 作設計，瑞昱預期
WiFi7 滲透率將由 2026 年的 30%攀升至 40%。WiFi 聯盟計畫 WiFi8 將於 06/2027
完成認證測試，並將於 09/2027 前正式啟動 WiFi8 認證，瑞昱預期 2H27 公司
WiFI8 產品可望步入量產，以利客戶搭載 WiFi8 產品更廣泛於 2028 年上市。(3) 
光通訊專注 112G/224G SerDes 開發(4nm 製程)，100G 光模組控制晶片將以雲
端、資料中心與電信網路為主要著墨項目，明年量產規模提升，並且 400G/800G 
PAM4 DSP預計 2H27 推出。(4) 電動車成長持續帶動瑞昱車載乙太網路進展，瑞
昱預期新車搭載乙太網路的滲透率已超過 50%。(5) 中長期邊緣 AI 業務(包括車載
/機器人/IOT)與高效能運算 (比如瑞昱導入乙太網路 /交換器/xPON/SSD/IO 橋接
器/USB hub/光通訊)，目標 2030 年營收比重達 30%。 
中立轉買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控管，
故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年營收 1466 億元(+19%YoY)，毛利率
47.91%，稅後獲利 157.9 億元(+7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 1594 億元(+9%YoY)，毛利率 48.37%，稅後獲利 181.4 億
元(+15%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力(或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

Valuation: We increase our 12m TP to NT$7,100 from NT$7,000 previously, following 
our upward earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x 
(unchanged; +1.8 stdv above 5 yr avg. fwd P/E) applied to our FY2H27E-1H28E EPS 
(unchanged). We reiterate our Buy rating on MediaTek.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - MediaTek (2454.TW) 
Valuation: Our 12m TP of NT$7,100 is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (1.8 stdv 
above its 5-year trading average) applied to our 2H27E-1H28E EPS. 
Key risks to our views: (1) Weaker-than-expected end demand especially with 
smartphones, (2) Higher foundry cost impacting its margin outlook, (3) Intensifying 
competition that would result in reduced profitability, and (4) Slower ramp in ASIC would 
result in changes in operating leverage

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide a 3Q26 results preview on Sunon, which plans to 
release its 3Q26 results on 6 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call as we 
expect the 4Q26 and 2027 outlook for its telecom, servers, industrial and 
auto segments to remain intact with both gross and operating margin set to 
expand. We now forecast a net profit CAGR of 28.2% over 2026-28E from 
21.2% previously (see our flash, 28 August).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. Sunon’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD6,143m (+13.4% QoQ and +24.5% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 97.2% and 102.2%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD6,319m, +16.6% QoQ and +28.1% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus estimates for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 32.8% (vs. 31.3% in 2Q26 and 31.3% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of 31.9%. We forecast an operating 
profit of TWD1,115m (+33.3% QoQ and +45.0% YoY), 3.0% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD1,082m and an operating 
margin of 18.1% (vs. 15.4% in 2Q26 and 15.6% in 3Q25). Overall, we 
forecast a net profit of TWD901m (+30.5% QoQ, +29.7% YoY), 1.9% 
higher than the consensus estimate of TWD884m. We forecast 4Q26 
revenue to grow 7.5% QoQ, and 1Q27 revenue to grow 1.4% QoQ 
considering its ASIC and GPU projects.

Rising liquid cooling revenue and EC fan exposure in 2027E underpin 
its gross margin expansion. We expect Sunon’s cold plate products (ie, 
enterprise and GPU clients), pump and CDU to be the key revenue drivers 
with certification also underway for CSP clients. We expect revenue from 
EC fans to reach 10% in 2027E for the industrial segment. We revise up 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-13%, mainly on higher revenue, gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD265 (from TWD236), still based on a target PER of 18x vs. its past-
5-year PER range of 6-25x, on the 2027E EPS, given solid earnings 
visibility from intact demand in the server, telecom, industrial and auto 
segments. We believe its dividend yield of 5.6-9.2% over 2026-28E will 
offer downside support for its share price. Downside risks: worse-than-
expected gross-margin expansion and AI server development progress.

How we differ: We are one of only two overseas brokers covering the 
stock. Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-12% above the Bloomberg consensus, 
likely due to our more aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating 
margin estimates.

Company profile 
Founded in 1980 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2000, Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry 
(Sunon) is one of the top-4 fan and motor providers in the world, along with Delta Electronics, Nidec and 
ebm-papst. Its major clients are Apple, Dell, Lenovo, Bosch, Siemens and Tesla. Its applications include 
IT & OE, telecoms & servers, industrial & medical equipment, home applications, distribution, autos and 
LED lighting.

### Page 5

5

Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry (2421 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 8 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Sunonwealth are the same individual; Sunonwealth's board is led
by the Hung family. We see no issues with the company’s board. We view the chairman leading
the R&D team as a sign of the company’s continuing focus on developing new products to
achieve a long-term competitive advantage.
Capital management 2 Sunonwealth's dividend payout ratio was 83-88% in 2015-18, but it decreased to 60-69% during 2022-25 due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during 2023-25.
E Materials sourcing & efficiency 1
Sunonwealth’s sourcing of raw materials has been relatively stable (including IC, copper, plastic,
aluminium and steel), and we also note that Sunonwealth abides by RoHS regulations and
relevant international standards (ISO14001, IECQ QC080000, ISO9001) for its material sourcing
activities. With the ongoing ESG trend, its clients may move towards a more energy-efficient
production cycle, which will likely benefit Sunonwealth’s energy-efficient products.
E Product design & lifecycle management 2
Sunonwealth has improved its product designs to stronger but lighter form factors. Besides, we
have observed more green projects utilising Sunonwealth's capabilities. Thus, we believe
Sunonwealth has strong product design and lifecycle management capabilities to address the
ongoing changing business environment.
S Product quality & safety 2 Sunonwealth has its own Quality Strategy Centre to evaluate quality issues. With international standards in place, we believe Sunonwealth has taken relevant measures to address product quality and safety issues.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部調升世芯-KY 的投資建議為強力買進，目標價上修至 6850
元，基於以下五點理由：(1)3Q26 雖受到供應鏈緊張影響，營收仍
持續創高，(2)4Q26 營收將持續攀升，(3)27 年 CSP 3 代晶片貢獻
營收幅度上修，(4)新增 AI 晶片客戶，有望在 27 年下半年貢獻約
10 億美元營收，(5)CSP 4 代晶片更加確立，28 年營收更高層級的
營收，且費用控管得宜，OPM 純度持續增加。綜合以上，維持26 年
EPS 預估為 120.31 元(原估120.3 元，+65.5%YOY)， 上修27 年 EPS
預估為 198.3 元(原估 178.99 元，+64.8%YOY)，及預估 28 年 EPS
為 341.95 元(+72.5%YOY)。雖然 1H26 營運動能表現不佳，但最差
狀況確實已過，目前供應鏈因素逐步排除，且在 27 年 CSP 客戶的
訂單配比增加 ， 且已經證明世芯為北美CSP 客戶唯一後段設計服務
供應商的角色，新增客戶產品也將在 27 年貢獻，先前股價修正幅
度較大，故上修建議至強力買進，目標價上修至 6850 元(27 年
EPS*35XPER OR 28 年EPS*20XPER)。

重點摘要：

1. 3Q26 受到供應鏈影響，惟營收仍維持高檔：  
世芯-KY 3Q26 營收為 261.9 億元(原估 284.5 億元)，成長
242.8%QOQ，成長298.7%YOY，小幅不如原先預期主要係因
載板及封裝產能稀缺等負面影響，如產能有爭取夠將有望
增加約 1 億美元之營收，就與先前預估相當。毛利率下滑
至 17.95%，與原先預估 17.5%相差不大，主要是部分 NRE
有遞延認列影響。費用10.7 億元 ，稍微高於於預估，不過
整體稅後淨利仍達到 32.51 億元(原估 33.09 億元)，成長
98.5%QOQ，成長145%YOY，EPS 37.67 元(原估 39.28 元)，
差異來自於營收規模。

2. 4Q26 營收將續創新高： 
展望 4Q26，雖然在法說會上公司未提及財測數字預估，不
過 CSP 客戶 3nm 案件持續放量，先前有供應端材料不順的
影響，與供應商及客戶偕同持續排除下，推估營收有望續
創新高 。 故研究部維持4Q26 營收預估為 334.5 億元 ， 成長
27.7%QOQ，成長 607.6%YOY，毛利率與先前預估相當的
17.5%，稅後淨利預估為 40.66 億元(原估 39.1 億元)，成
長 25.1%QOQ，成長 174.2%YOY，EPS 47.11 元(原估 45.35
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CSP 3 代晶片推估 26 年貢獻 15 億美金，27 年確定上修 
法說會雖然因法規及與客戶 NDA 的關係，皆無提到CSP 客
戶三代晶片能夠貢獻的營收幅度，不過從營收可以看出，
先前規劃的投片持續順利產出，26 年維持貢獻 15 億美元
營收不變。展望27 年，先前預估較為保守，主要係因3nm
產能稀缺，但此次法說會公司表示，在研發上持續配合客
戶 ， 而客戶依賴度提升狀況下 ，27 年的訂單配比有所提升 ，
產能也有所確保，故上修成長幅度至 100%YOY 以上，先前
僅預估 60-70%YOY 左右。

4. CSP 4 代晶片訂單更加確立，且新增另一客戶： 
另外在 4 代晶片方面，法說會當中有提到 2nm 案件將在
4Q26 TAPE OUT ，且再次宣告也只有一家後段設計服務供
應商 ， 包含IO DIE 及連結部分 ， 且公司也扮演整合晶片的
角色， 以時間點來看，將有望在4Q27 做小規模的量產， 且
僅有 ANNAPURNA 及世芯有在台積電投片該晶片的權力。 
此次在法說會有提到，公司新增另外一個大客戶，公司未
透露細節，但非GOOGLE，客戶為美國客戶，晶片就是與AI
相關(未指出是推論還是訓練)，且該晶片在 27 年下半年
會貢獻營收 ， 推估營收規模將達到10 億美元 。 而世芯與該
客戶的生意將具有延續性。 
故綜合以上，世芯對於 28 年營收規模期待將有更大的跳
升，且 OPM 將會往 HIGH TEENS 邁進。

5. 26年約略維持預估，27年預估上修，28年粗估高度成長： 
3Q26世芯財報約略符合預期，雖然有供應鏈相關問題，不
過4Q26營收仍將續創新高，故維持 26年營收預估為714.6
億元(原估737億元)，EPS 120.31元(原估120.3元)。展望
27年，CSP客戶三代晶片貢獻全年 ， 加上爭取到的投片及訂
單配比上修，另外新增客戶也將貢獻營收，故上修營收為
1529.3億元(原估1335億元)，EPS上修至198.3 元(原估
178.99元)。28年在CSP 4代晶片開始貢獻下，研究部粗估
1Q28小幅量產，2Q28開始大幅放量，推估28年營收將達到
2593億元，成長72.5%YOY，EPS預估再跳升至341.95元。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

기아 (000270)

Company Brief 
자동차/모빌리티 
Analyst 김준성 
joonsung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 배현서 
hyeonseo.bae@meritz.co.kr 배당은 지켜질 것  
3Q26 지배주주 순이익, 컨센서스 -38% 하회 
매출 31.6조원 (+10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스에 부합했다. 출고 판매 (+10% YoY), 소
매 판매 (+8% YoY) 모두 높은 성장을 기록하며, 외형 확대에 기여했다. 
영업이익 1.82조원 (+24% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -25% 하회했다. 전년 동기 대미 
관세 25% 부과에 따른 기저효과 +5,330억원 (2025년 11월부터 15%로 하향)이 
존재했고 기말 환율 (원/달러 -12% QoQ 강세) 변동에 따른 판매보증 충당부채 환
입 +6,700억원을 인식했음에도 불구하고, 신규 출시 저가 BEV 모델들의 판매 확대
에 따른 전사 평균 판매가격 하락  할인 경쟁 심화  원재료 및 운송비 상승으로 부
진한 실적을 기록했다. 
지배주주 순이익 1.29조원 (-10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -38% 하회했다. 영업 실적 
부진과 함께 원화 강세에 의한 외화자산 평가손실 및 관계사 실적 악화에 따른 지분
법 이익 감소 (-36% YoY)가 더해진 결과다.

6,000원 이상 배당 유지 전망 
4Q26 이후 실적 방향도 먹구름이 가득하다. 원/달러 환율의 YoY 강세 반영이 시작
된다. 미국 시장 내 경쟁력 확보를 위해 가장 중요한 원/엔 환율의 방향성 또한 비우
호적 흐름이 지속 중이다. 내수 시장을 포함해 전세계 주요 시장 내 중국산 차량과의 
경쟁 강도도 높아지고 있다. 원유 가격 뿐만 아니라 알루미늄, 구리 등 핵심 원재료 
가격의 상승 반영도 이어지고 있다. 
2026년 연간 지배주주 순이익 6.9조원 (-9% YoY) 을 전망한다. 주주환원 총액은 축
소될 것이다. 다만 자사주 매입을 줄이더라도 배당은 전년 수준을 지킬 것으로 기대
한다. 참고로 2025년 실적에 기반한 주주환원은 자사주 매입 0.9조원, 배당 2.6조원 
(주당 6,800원)이었으며, 주주환원율은 48%였다. 현대차그룹은 Boston Dynamics
에 대한 증자를 이어가고 있다. 정의선 회장도 마찬가지다. 그리고 기아의 배당은 현
대차  현대글로비스의 배당과 함께 증자 재원으로 활용되고 있다. 6,000 원 이상의 
배당 유지를 기대하며, 6% 이상 시가 배당 수익률 제시를 통해 주가 하방 경직성 확
보가 가능하다고 판단한다. 기업가치 상승 재개를 위해서는 기존 차량 제조  판매 사
업을 넘어 피지컬 데이터 디바이스 제공 사업 진출 가시성 확보가 필요하다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 11. 2

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

Document 278: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

00

삼성전자 (005930/KS) 
현저한 저평가 
3Q26 영업이익 106 조원 예상 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익을 106조원 (+18% QoQ)으로 예상한다. 이는 기존 당사 
전망치 111 조원 대비 4.5% 하향된 것이다. 원/달러 환율 가정을 기존 1,450원에서 
1,400원으로 3.4% 하향, MX/NW 사업부의 적자 폭을 확대 반영 (-0.5조원 -> -
1.4조원)했기 때문이다. 주가의 핵심인 메모리 판가는 DRAM +17%, NAND +15%
로 오히려 예상을 소폭 상회할 것으로 예상된다. 출하는 수요 강세에도 불구하고 재고 
부족, HBM 확대에 따른 제한적 공급 여력으로 DRAM +4%, NAND +7%의 기존 예
상을 유지한다. HBM은 분기 최대 출하를 경신하기 시작할 것이다. 또한 AI 강세로 파
운드리 수주 및 가동률의 동반 상승이 지속 중이다. 성과 충당금 반영 등에 따라 단기 
흑자전환 가시성은 제한적이나, DS 사업부 전체적으로 강화되고 있는 국면이다.

2027 년 568 조원 (+55% YoY) 전망 유지. 환율 하락 반영 
SK증권은 삼성전자의 2027년 영업이익 전망치를 568조원으로 유지한다. 원/달러 환
율 가정을 기존 1,440원에서 1,340원으로 7% 하향했음에도, 실적 전망을 유지하는 
이유는 AI 메모리 수요 강세 속 공급 부족 심화, LTA에 따른 Dual market 효과에 의
한 Commodity 메모리 가격 강세, 2027년 HBM 판가 강세 (+103% YoY) 와 출하 
강세, 수익성 회복의 동반, 파운드리 적자 폭 축소의 본격화 전망 때문이다. 2027년 메
모리 출하는 DRAM +22%, NAND +16%, 판가는 DRAM +38%, NAND +28%
를 예상한다. 시장에서 우려하는 메모리 가격 하락, 감익 시나리오는 없을 것이다.

차별적 실적과 주주환원 본격화. 현저한 저평가 
SK증권은 메모리 업황과 삼성전자의 주가가 현저히 저평가 되어있다고 판단한다. 삼성
전자의 2027E P/E는 4.2X에 불과하며, 이익 창출력은 글로벌 최상위권이다. 주주환
원 정책 또한 기존의 FCF 50% 환원이 향후에도 유지될 것으로 가정한다면, 2027년 
193조원, 2028년 222조원으로 현재 시가총액 (우선주 포함) 대비 각각 11%, 13% 
수준에 육박한다. 성장을 위한 투자를 확대하고 있음에도, 대규모의 환원이 가능하다. 
LTA는 AI 시대 메모리 수요의 질과 실적 가시성을 제고시키며, 이는 주주환원의 지속
가능성을 의미한다. 메모리 가격의 높은 기저, LTA 물량의 계약 가격 적용을 감안하면, 
가격 상승률 자체는 안정화될 것이다. 다만 실적 안정성, 가시성, Duration을 확보하기 
시작했다는 점이 훨씬 중요한 가치이며, 아직 시장의 Playbook에 없을 뿐이다. 시대는 
변하고 있고, 시장도 변해야 한다. 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 610,000원을 유지한다. 
2026-10-06

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

3Q26 Review: 악재의 중첩, 
4분기까지 지켜보자 
최정환 CFA

jeonghwan.choi@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 1,640,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
739,000 
방위산업

◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
◼ 컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
◼ 4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
4Q26E-2Q27E 지상방산 및 항공우주 EBITDA에 Target 12MF EV/EBITDA 25.0x 
적용, 상장 자회사 가치 SOTP 통해 목표주가 산출. 3Q26 실적 아쉬우나 투자포
인트에는 변함 없어 멀티플은 변경 없이 적용. 27년 실적 추정치 상향에 따라 본
업 가치 상승, 26년 스페인 자주포 양산 및 미국 자주포 시제 사업자로 선정되며 
현지화 전략이 유효했음을 증명. 3Q26 실적에 대한 우려를 소화하며 글로벌 피어
들과의 멀티플 격차 축소를 전망함

컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 6.1조원(-6.0% YoY), 영업이익 4,520억원(-46.2% YoY, 
OPM 7.4%)으로 컨센서스인 매출액 7.4조원 및 영업이익 1.1조원을 크게 하회
하는 실적 시현. 하회의 주요 요인으로는 3Q26 필리조선소 적자폭 확대, 지상
방산 부문 실적이 부진한 영향. 지상방산 부문의 경우 매출액 1.2조원(-41.0% 
YoY), 영업이익 220억원(-96.2% YoY, OPM 1.7%)을 시현했는데, 폴란드 관련 
매출이 부재했던 1Q26 영업이익률 16.9%보다도 낮은 실적을 보임. 이는 국내
외 사업 모두에서 악재가 겹쳐 발생했던 것으로 보이는데 국내 사업에서는 마
진율이 낮은 사업이 매출로 인식되었고 수출 사업에서는 폴란드 매출 부재 뿐 
아니라 이집트, 호주 K9 자주포 매출 인식 또한 예상 대비 부진해 고정비 레버
리지 효과로 3Q26 영업이익률이 크게 훼손된 것으로 추정. 이외 대전사업장 복
구에 따른 비용, 기타 사업 충당금 등 총 500억원 수준의 일회성 비용이 발생했
으며 영업외손익에서는 원화 강세에 따른 환손실이 주로 반영되었음

4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향 
1Q26에서 3Q26까지 이뤄지지 않았던 폴란드 EC2 K9 자주포 30문 인도는 
4Q26에 집중될 것으로 보이며 폴란드 EC1 천무 또한 기존 가이던스였던 26년 
천무 40대에서 60대로 인도 대수 상향이 이뤄짐. 이에 따라 4Q26 지상방산 영
업이익률은 22.8%로 회복을 전망. 27년에는 폴란드 K9 자주포 및 천무 인도 대
수 증가로 26년 수출 비중 58.0%에서 77.2%로 증가, 이에 따라 영업이익률 또
한 매출 믹스 개선에 따라 19.8%에서 28.0%로 개선을 전망

두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review]  매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PP A 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PP A 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR은 2035년 운전을 목표로 2028년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031년 출하 물량은 2028년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82만원에서 64만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

Document 311: AI boom or doom_ what will superintelligence feel like and when will we get there_ (2026-10-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI boom or doom_ what will superintelligence feel like and when will we get there_ (2026-10-12).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 20
Status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

AI boom or doom: what will 
superintelligence feel like and  
when will we get there?

1 
Authors 
Adrian Cox 
Research Analyst

Azeem Azhar, Exponential View 
Expert Collaborator

October 12, 2026 
The recent panic about rogue AI agents collaborating in swarms to take over the 
internet and threaten human existence has, more than occasionally, lacked 
nuance. But it does reflect how artificial intelligence is progressing in creative 
and surprising ways, and at a pace that seems uncontrollable. 
For some in the industry, it is evidence that the technology is following a path of 
accelerating self-improvement that will lead to artificial general intelligence (AGI) 
and, perhaps, to utopia or catastrophe. 
Former Google CEO Eric Schmidt has sketched out a “San Francisco 
Consensus” based on a belief in scaling laws, with ever larger AI models 
becoming an input into their own improvement; superintelligence by the end of 
the decade; and transformative AI systems outperforming humans on many 
tasks. This loop would lead to exponential growth in scientific advancement, 
financial returns and human progress. And crucially, AGI, and then 
superintelligence, would merely be milestones in a process, far from its climax. 
If that were the case, superintelligence could make much of the cognitive work 
that dominates the world economy much cheaper and change the ways in which 
firms compete. But its economic effects will depend on how businesses and 
customers respond. 
In this paper, we analyse what the San Francisco Consensus means for investors 
and enterprises in the years to come and what could accelerate – or slow down – 
its timeline of AGI by 2030. 
1. What would superintelligence look and feel like? 
2. Can we get there theoretically? 
3. Can we get there practically? 
4. What happens if we don’t get there? The base case 
The question is urgent. 
And it is complicated by reports of AI models autonomously accessing the 
internet and hacking other companies; a public backlash over the energy-
intensive data centres springing up as part of a historic $1 trillion a year 
investment boom; and dire warnings of threats to life if superintelligent AI runs 
roughshod over its human creators to achieve its own goals.

### Page 2

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

1. What would superintelligence look and feel like? 
1.1 What are AGI and superintelligence? 
Artificial general intelligence (AGI) is a simple milestone scribbled in pencil: an AI 
system that has surpassed human capabilities. Superintelligence is the 
continuum beyond that point. It is not merely systems that exceed the best 
human performance across one cognitive task or another, but system s that 
exceed human potential across every cognitive task. 
The San Francisco Consensus takes this further: the intelligence must be able to 
produce more intelligence. The key to getting there is so -called recursive self -
improvement, where the AI is able to autonomously design and improve itself in 
a way that compounds exponentially. A first step has maybe already been taken, 
with OpenAI saying recently that AI is now writing approximately 80 percent of 
the company’s code. 
There is significant disagreement on how we will be able to identify AGI or 
superintelligence when we see it, let alone what comes afterwards.  
OpenAI’s Charter defines AGI in economic terms, as “highly autonomous systems 
that outperform humans at the most economically valuable work”. Google 
researchers proposed a five -step ladder based on performance and generality, 
with level 5 “superhuman” for a system that outperforms 100% of humans. And 
philosopher Nick Bostrom, who got the ball rolling in 2014 with his influential 
book “Superintelligenc e”, defined it as “any intellect that greatly exceeds the 
cognitive performance of humans in virtually all domains of interest”. 
It’s also a moving target.  
In August, OpenAI CEO Sam Altman said the company would have an internal 
system that he would call AGI by later this year but subsequently told a podcast 
that “at best it’s a very poorly defined term. I was going to say it’s like an irrelevant 
marketing term”. Yet his No. 2 Greg Brockman described the new Astra model at 
the start of September as being potentially the moment that “AGI was created”. 
For this report, we have chosen to focus on a system that outperforms essentially 
all humans across essentially all cognitive tasks, including the development of AI 
itself.  
Under our scenario, by 2030 the best performing AI models would have the 
qualities of “superintelligence”, that is better than human experts in all domains. 
These would be frontier models: and using them would feel exactly like bringing 
the best human experts in a room to solve whatever business problem you faced.

And the equivalents of today’s frontier models would be available, quite literally, 
everywhere, compressed enough to run on every phone, tablet or laptop 
computer.  
In early 2026, we already see early examples of what highly capable AI can do. 
They give a flavour for a world with superintelligence even if the reality would be 
a leap beyond, both quantitatively and qualitatively. At that point, 
superintelligence would mark a regime break, not a faster version of what we 
already have. Rather than blindly doing what humans specify, but just a bit better, 
it would specify the task itself.

### Page 3

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

Figure 1: What changes when cognitive work gets abundantly cheaper?

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

Meanwhile cost may be less and less of a constraint as ever greater machine 
intelligence becomes cheaply available. Of measured technologies, no 
technology has got cheaper faster than AI has, almost halving in price every 
quarter for the past three years on a like-for-like basis, or 13 times per year, Epoch 
data show. 
If these systems can be supplied widely, firms will be able to buy more of the 
complex, expensive and rare analysis, design and engineering kinds of services 
than they do today. Activities that were previously uneconomic could become 
viable, and in a competitive market, savings could pass to customers. The main 
driver will then be whether demand grows fast enough to preserve margins. 
However, superior capability, doesn’t, on its own, automatically lead to the 
adoption of cheap, abundant intelligence. Tasks that do not yet yield to super-

### Page 4

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

intelligence, perhaps because they require real world-coordination, will therefore 
end up commanding significant premia. 
At that point, cognitive labour stops being scarce and the bottleneck becomes 
the physical and practical assets that cannot be conjured by up more compute, 
such as electricity, land, steel and permits. 
1.2 Capability and diffusion 
If AI becomes capable of improving itself, the technology may compound faster 
and faster. The supply of machine intelligence could grow faster than business 
and physical infrastructure can accommodate.  
This balance between capability and diffusion creates different scenarios.

Figure 2: The balance between capabilities and diffusion

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

Lower capability, constrained diffusion: where we are now 
Progress is steady and sector-by-sector. This is how technology usually diffuses, 
according to researchers for the New York Fed back in 2003, who tracked 20 
major technologies across leading industrial economies from 1788 and found “a 
pattern of trickle-down diffusion that is remarkably robust across technologies”. 
Lower capability, high diffusion: the cloud transition 
This is faster because the complementary investment is small, with no retooling 
and no skills at scale required. Amazon Web Services launched the modern cloud 
in 2006, offering scalable computing and storage infrastructure. Adoption grew 
and then accelerated over the pandemic. Almost all enterprises now use it  in 
some form.

### Page 5

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

Higher capability, lower diffusion: the overhang 
This is theoretically possible and arguably where we are today in narrow domains. 
The technology would be applied in some sectors of the economy, while other 
sectors would move at a slower speed.  Superintelligent or not, it risks being 
bogged down by physical constraints, bureaucracy and human inertia. 
Higher capability, higher diffusion: superintelligence 
In this scenario, capability becomes so high that it changes the dynamics of 
diffusion itself. AI generates not just better AI (so -called recursive self -
improvement, or RSI) but also speeds up the integration process. 
Rapid AI diffusion could work two ways. 
In the first, AI could be enlisted to do integration work itself: mapping processes, 
rebuilding data work, retraining staff, writing the control and audit layer, and so 
on. The problem with this is that absorbing and recreating undocumented 
knowledge is impossible by definition, at least for a task-based superintelligence.  
The second way that higher capability AI could be rapidly diffused is much more 
competitive – and disruptive. Rather than incumbents slowly converting AI, 
perhaps using it for some of the hard work of integration, they could be instead 
displaced by new firms that have been built AI-native from day one. New entrants 
will never have to incur integration costs because there is nothing to integrate. 
Incumbents will need to change – fast – or die.  
Change could also be forced by earlier adopters in the supply chain, as happened 
with one company after another adopting electronic data interchange (EDI) to 
link with its suppliers or customers, or by a migration of capital towards early 
adopters, reprici ng the cost of capital for slow -coaches and prodding them to 
move faster. 
1.3 The forcing mechanism: change or die 
A company that uses superintelligence may outperform one that doesn’t. 
Firms using superintelligence will likely have substantial cost and revenue -
harvesting advantages. Every firm will therefore have to adopt superintelligence 
or retrench to a smaller set of offerings. And any business line of lasting value 
abandoned by a no n-adopter will be taken over by an adopter. Customer inertia 
will provide a safe harbour for a limited time only.  
The analogy here is electricity. There came a point in the deployment of 
electricity where firms in every sector, from entertainment to finance, from 
healthcare to transport, had to adopt the technology to remain relevant.  
Under this scenario, wait -and-see will not be an option. There will be no stable 
“non-adoption” alternative. Sufficiently high capabilities will force a tipping point 
of rapid diffusion and competitive repricing. Firms using yesterday’s production 
model will either have to change, choose a different market or die.  
Of course, caveats apply.  
Customers may not change behaviour immediately. They may even prefer 
companies which limit their use of AI, in the same way that some customers 
choose to avoid factory-produced food.

### Page 6

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

The paradox that Nobel Prize-winning economist Robert Solow observed in 1987, 
that “you can see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics”, 
is likely to still apply for a while – even with superintelligence. 
The reason is that the productivity J -curve identified by Stanford University AI 
economy guru Erik Brynjolfsson et al is unavoidable. In that scenario, the drag of 
early intangible investments to integrate general purpose technologies means 
that productivity initially appears to slow before rapidly accelerating.

Figure 3: Productivity J-curve: slow then fast

Source: Brynjolfsson E, Rock D, Syverson C, “The Productivity J-Curve” (2018, revised 2020), Deutsche Bank Research

Furthermore, “change or die” would require abundant capital and low regulatory 
entry points for nimble new entrants. But in practice, highly regulated areas such 
as banking, utilities, pharmaceuticals and defence are tough nuts to crack. 
Existing contracts and licences, high switching costs and decades of depreciated 
capital investment and infrastructure would shield incumbents at least for a 
healthy transition period. 
For an extreme example from the real world of better, cheaper technology failing 
to force change, take Pakistan’s 3.6 -gigawatt imported coal power plant fleet 
installed in the mid -2010s. In that case, it came with expensive take -or-pay 
liabilities which wi ll continue to run for decades, even though changes like 
cheaper rooftop solar have cut utilisation to below 20 percent.  
So: superintelligence will not force adoption everywhere. It will hit hardest in 
contestable sectors, say, high -volume customer services, routine software 
engineering and freelance creative work, and widen the gap between them and 
protected ones. Forced adoption will be about relative value, not an apocalypse.

### Page 7

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

1.4 What survives and what it means for portfolios 
Because AI sits on an aggressive learning curve, its price on an equivalent quality 
basis is plummeting. In a world of recursive super intelligence this is likely to 
continue. Its price will fall to the cost of compute.  
What will retain its value will be whatever the model cannot replicate: 
1. Proprietary data 
2. The right to operate , particularly in heavily regulated sectors such as 
healthcare and finance. 
3. Sticky customer relationships,  such as those where mission -critical 
workflows and databases are deeply entwined. 
4. Excludable physical assets, such as machinery and land. 
5. In addition, humans have proven to have unlimited wants.  Each shift in 
technological capabilities or cultural norms has delivered new needs to 
be met. Some will be needs we can articulate today, such as originality or 
empathy, but new ones are likely to arise.  
If this forcing mechanism holds, firms will separate into two “bimodal” groups: 
adopters and casualties, with very little in between. 
This dispersion of performance may create challenges for investors. The four 
years since the launch of ChatGPT in November 2022 have seen the stocks of 
semiconductor and semiconductor equipment makers surge almost seven times 
while those of software makers  have only risen by 80 percent. Conventional  
diversification and volatility-based allocation may become ineffective strategies.

Figure 4: Total return for S&P 500 semiconductor vs software stocks since 
launch of ChatGPT on Nov 30, 2022

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research

### Page 8

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 8

Diversification normally works because idiosyncratic risks are unrelated and 
average each other out. Here they would not: with one variable that overrides 
everything else, spreading capital across more names would just spread exposure 
rather than diluting it. 
Investors will need to consider adapting their approach in three ways: 
1. Classify investments as adopters or casualties rather than position off 
averages.  
• Under a normal distribution, errors are symmetric and partially 
cancel each other out. But under bimodality they do not: to put a firm 
in one category or another means getting it completely right or 
wrong. Position size should track confidence in the classification. 
2. Avoid reading calm price action as low risk.  
• Trailing volatility measures dispersion that has already been realised. 
Bimodal separation is a jump, where the two modes look identical 
right until the moment when they don’t. A classic example is Chegg, 
an educational software maker which traded like a normal small-cap 
stock until one day in May 2023 when it halved on a single day after 
management linked slowing growth in new customers to ChatGPT. 
3. Averaging down, ie  scooping up shares whose price has fallen, may no 
longer be prudent. 
2. Can we get there theoretically? 
2.1 Scaling laws 
Scaling laws have provided the blueprint for improving AI over the past decade. 
It was a relationship between the amount of data, the amount of compute and 
the complexity of an AI model somewhat predictably informed how powerful a 
model would become. Google DeepMind’s Chinchilla model in 2022 showed that 
you could compensate for having a smaller model by using substantially more 
data. 
Scaling is older than the chatbot. In 1950 Claude Shannon built Theseus, a 
mechanical mouse that learned its way through a maze by trial and error. Epoch 
AI reckons it took about 40 operations of compute, known as FLOPs, to train. Grok 
4, trained in 2025, took roughly ten trillion trillion times as much. Across those 25 
orders of magnitude, more compute has brought more capability.

• A falling price may be information that a business model is obsolete 
rather than an opportunity.

### Page 9

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

Figure 5: Computation used to train notable AI systems (petaFLOP): log scale

Source: Epoch AI (citing estimates from AI literature), Our World in Data, Deutsche Bank Research

It is the robustness of this relationship that has made larger investments in 
compute capex a sine qua non for AI companies. 
However, this is not a law of physics and nothing guarantees it will continue . 
Indeed, in the ChatGPT era, five different approaches to scaling have been 
applied. Older strategies yield worse results, and new techniques, such as post -
training refinements, have been developed. The consensus amongst American 
and Chinese AI labs is that these will hold.  
While this may be a consensus amongst the commercial labs, there is scientific 
disagreement that apply ing scaling laws to today’s large -language models can 
achieve superintelligence. 
Yann LeCun, a Turing Award winner and formerly Meta's chief AI scientist, is blunt: 
"LLMs are not a path to superintelligence or even human-level intelligence”. Even 
inside the labs, the recipe is contested. Demis Hassabis of Google DeepMind has 
said he expects "one or two more big breakthroughs" to be needed "in addition to 
scaling". Ilya Sutskever, a co -founder and former chief scientist of OpenAI, has 
said the field is going "back to the age of research again, just with big computers". 
This is a scientific argument we are not in a position to evaluate , but for the 
purposes of our scenario, we will side with the labs’ position. 
For the moment, there is strong evidence that capabilities have in fact 
accelerated over the past three years, as reasoning models have outpaced non -
reasoning models by two or three times, according to Epoch data.

### Page 10

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 10

2.2 Recursive self-improvement: AI improving AI 
A cocktail-party definition of recursive self-improvement (RSI) is AI that improves 
itself. The reality is that it can be used in two ways: 
1. Self-improving agents that get better at using themselves: showing 
smarter use of prompts, tools, memory and workflows. The model -
building process is untouched. 
2. True RSI improves the process that builds the models: designing 
experiments, curating data, optimising algorithms and designing chips in 
a compounding loop. 
The former is like a factory worker who gets better at the job by learning the quirks 
of the machinery, getting organised and making fewer mistakes . The latter is like 
the worker redesigning the machine tools and then using those to build the next 
generation of machines. 
True RSI is what OpenAI’s chief scientist Jacob Pachoki referred to last month in 
a blog post about  “An Alien Mind ”. “Automated AI research is a more dramatic 
form of scaling intelligence with compute,” he wrote. “And of course as a part of 
it, AI will improve the computational substrate itself,” he added. Pachoki 
represents a broadly held consensus view amongst America n and Chinese labs 
that RSI is both possible and necessary for AI development. 
• OpenAI (June 2026): “AI doing AI research will become the determining 
factor of the pace of progress within the next few years.” 
• Google DeepMind’s Demis Hassabis (May 2026): “All the leading labs are 
quite focused on [RSI]…” Humanity is standing in the “foothills of the 
singularity”. 
• Alibaba CEO Eddie Wu (Sept 2026): RSI is “a concrete path forward,” with 
models autonomously designing experiments and driving a cycle of self-
evolution. 
For now, self -improving agents are the AI du jour. Noisy claims about the vast 
majority of coding being done by agents do not always stand up in the light of day 
from companies still hiring human coders at breakneck pace, albeit no longer to 
do the most mundane parts of the job.  
A better metric may be METR’s task -completion time horizon. This is measure of 
the complexity of software tasks that an AI agent can complete on its own, using 
the baseline of how long it would take a human expert to finish the task, at a 50 
percent success rate. So far the most advanced models have reached about 16 
hours, up from OpenAI’s GPT-4’s four minutes in March 2023.

### Page 11

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 11

Figure 6: Task horizon of software tasks that leading frontier models can complete 50% of the time (log scale)

Source: METR, Deutsche Bank Research

But software engineers can hold off on updating their resumes a little longer: 
there is a long road between 50 percent accuracy and the 99 percent or 100 
percent needed for serious enterprise use at scale, with the horizon potentially 
getting further away the nearer you get. Also METR notes that measurements 
above 16 hours are unreliable with the current task suite. As is the case across AI, 
the instrument is running out before the question is settled.  
More broadly, there are significant limitations to RSI:  
1. Research is clock-bound.  
• A frontier training run can take weeks on thousands of specialised chips, 
and researchers often cannot design the next experiment until the 
previous one has been completed.  
• That means that the speed of the loop is set by experiments completed 
per hour or day, not by ideas generated.  
• Compute is a hard ceiling that needs to be bought, buil t and connected 
to a grid. 
2. The real world defies verifiability.  
• The loop compounds only when the system can check its own output 
cheaply and automatically. 
• It is no coincidence that AI loves maths and coding. It is much harder to 
verify outputs over a long time horizon in the real world: does this drug 
work or does this strategy succeed?  
• RSI compounds fastest where the economy cares least and slows where 
the returns are largest.

### Page 12

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 12

3. Sequences are consequential.  
• Amdahl’s law finds that speed -up is capped when processes run in a 
sequence.  
• If you automate 90 percent, the residual 10 percent still limits you to a 
ten-times faster process. Nine women cannot produce a baby in one 
month.  
• The Usain Bolt of AI still has to run alongside the comparative tortoises 
of the training run itself, chip fabrication and real -world validation. Ten 
times faster is still not a regime break. 
This is borne out by METR’s series. Doubling every seven months is fast 
exponential growth but a take -off would show the doubling time itself – the 
second derivative – shrinking. Horizons are not (yet) exploding. 
Philosopher Toby Ord shows that recursive self -improvement can only reach a 
singularity if each cycle of improvement takes less time than the last, shrinking 
fast towards zero. He argues that real feedback loops have a floor: chips take time 
to make and models take time to train. A I progress could still outrun an 
exponential, but it would not go vertical. 
2.3 Scaling plus RSI: a match made in the race to AGI? 
No scaling and RSI means no short timeline for AGI. Without scaling, more 
compute buys nothing and capital is wasted. Without RSI, AI capability will 
continue to improve at the pace of flesh -and-blood human researchers, 
augmented by AI tools but not replac ed. Only with both will it be possible to 
produce a system that improves faster than the humans building it. Without one 
or the other, no quantity of chips, energy or capital will fix it. 
3. Can we get there practically? 
3.1 Infrastructure, chips and energy 
There is no consensus on the amount of training required to reach AGI. Estimates 
suggest that training 2026's top models required between 10^26 and 10^27 
FLOPs. If we hypothesise that an "AGI -like model" would require three orders of 
magnitude more training, this would require 10^30 FLOPs.   
Consider today’s US AI infrastructure, which runs to approximately 10^22 FLOPs. 
If all of this was conceivably used for a 10^30 FLOP training run, it would take 
nearly four years to complete.  
The physical infrastructure to practically support such a training run would 
require enormous power, perhaps around 6GW, about 50% more than the four 
Vogtle nuclear power plants recently activated in the US. The cost of the 
compute infrastructure would run to between $300bn and $500bn.  
Today’s advanced chips would simply not suffice. Back-of-the-envelope maths 
suggests that even using Nvidia’s most advanced Vera Rubin chips, training such 
a model in four months would require 7.8m chips and 26GW of facility power. 
That would cost $900bn today and a rental equivalent cost of $90bn.  
Of course, frontier compute is growing rapidly, and price -performance is 
declining. If current trends hold, a 2030 -era cluster capable of such a run would

### Page 13

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 13

need perhaps $200bn in computer and power hardware for a training run costing 
about $20bn.

Figure 7: Technology cost index over time (years since start of price decline)

Source: Epoch AI, Deutsche Bank Research

The hyperscalers are leading the charge towards AGI. Alphabet, Amazon, Meta, 
Microsoft and Oracle are set to invest almost $800bn in capital expenditure this 
year, mostly for ever more powerful data centres, and over $1 trillion a year 
through 2030 and beyond.

Figure 8: Hyperscaler capital expenditure (including estimates)

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research

### Page 14

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 14

By 2030, assuming no market retrenchment, this means additional $4 trillion to 
$6 trillion on AI compute capability invested. This infrastructure could 
accommodate a training run of 10^30 FLOPs with some comfort.  
But even if AI labs crack the secret sauce of RSI, and their finance chiefs are willing 
to invest in such large training runs, and hyperscalers are prepared to continue to 
build the infrastructure, the infrastructure needs to be built.  
Grid connection queues, turbine lead times and transmission build are measured 
in years, not the months it takes to assemble and air-freight the latest Nvidia chip. 
The lead time for substation transformers is currently as long as three years. 
Politics in the US has shifted towards slowing the pace of the AI build out and this 
force will run against the demands for larger data centres that would be essential 
for RSI.  
3.2 Capital 
Financing for AI has been abundant in the historic buildout of the past three years. 
However, it is still largely conditional not on revenue but on belief in a future 
payoff. 
RSI is a “key part of the investment thesis” in the “biggest scientific bet civilisation 
has ever made,” Google DeepMind’s chief strategy officer Jasjeet Sekhon said at 
a conference in August, The Information reported. “We have this danger we could 
hit an AI air pocket such that the expenditures happen but the revenues don’t 
show up.” 
An inflection point is approaching where the nature of investment changes from 
free cash flow and equity to debt, with all that entails in terms of exposure to 
rising rates and creditor appetite. The hyperscalers are reaching a point where 
capex is no long er covered by operating cashflow and where net debt exceeds 
earnings before interest, depreciation and amortisation (EBITDA). 
Alphabet, for example, posted its first quarterly free  cash outflow in nearly two 
decades in its most recent quarter, even as it lifted its full-year capex guidance to 
$205bn. It said it would continue to rent third -party capacity and reported a 
backlog of $514bn in contracted, not-yet-recognised data centre revenue. 
3.3 Data 
Data has been the new oil for some time now. But unfortunately for AI 
development it seems to be running out even faster. 
The effective stock of quality - and repetition -adjusted public human text was 
about 300 trillion tokens, with a vast 90 percent confidence interval of 100 trillion 
to 1,000 trillion, Epoch estimated in 2024. A token is roughly equivalent to three 
quarters of a word. By that count it was likely to be fully used between 2026 and 
2032.

### Page 15

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 15

Figure 9: Projections of stock of public text and data usage

Source: Epoch AI, Deutsche Bank Research

However, since then, rather than data dropping off a cliff, there has been a rise in 
synthetic data generation, transfer learning from data-rich domains such as video 
and telemetry, and data efficiency. While the data shortage has not been 
resolved, the immediate crisis has been averted. 
AI now makes much of its own training data. When DeepMind's AlphaGo became 
superhuman at playing the mind-bendingly complex board game Go, it did so not 
by playing human experts, but by playing millions and millions of games against 
itself. 
For many domains relating to coding and mathematical proofs where automatic 
checks are possible, such synthetic experience can move things forward.  That is 
the case with AI research, where the data may be the results of experiments, all 
of which are bought by compute. About 90% of OpenAI's 2024 research compute 
went on such experiments. 
And increasingly, people write the tests rather than the answers. By the summer 
of 2026, selling training data for practi ce tasks was a nearly $9bn revenue 
business. Firms such as Mercor hire experts to generate long tasks against which 
the AI can be trained. 
The challenge here is that trust in the checker becomes a scarce input.

### Page 16

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 16

As AI makes production cheaper, it also makes establishing that production is 
accurate or reliable more expensive.  Domains which can be checked 
mechanically, such as mathematics, will be opened up more quickly. But those 
which rely on expert verification, such as medicine, engineering or law, may 
progress more slowly. 
The likely implications are twofold: a slower rate of diffusion into economic 
domains that are less verifiable and a larger share of production costs devoted to 
assurance. 
Another issue is that s ystems that have to  grade themselves learn to game the 
process. T his was partially what happened when an OpenAI model escap ed a 
sandbox and hacked into a third-party website. Trust replaced data as a wall. 
There are also question marks over the quality of data that is recycled repeatedly 
through the system. Although humans continue to produce original content, half 
of internet articles are now primarily generated by AI, Graphite data show.

Figure 10: Human vs primarily AI-generated articles on the internet

Source: Graphite (citing 55.4k articles from Common Crawl examined by AI detectors), Deutsche Bank Research

3.4 An incomplete case 
This analysis shows that the San Francisco Consensus, which emphasises RSI and 
a short timeline to AGI, is neither predestined nor impossible.  
Rather it depends on whether the science is right, on whether scaling is all you 
need, and on whether the infrastructure and capital required for that scaling is 
deployed and accessible to the leading labs.  
There is, in other words, a possible rather than impossible, path to AGI, subject to 
those conditions.  
None of this proves that superintelligence will ever arrive. A buildup at this scale 
has few peacetime precedents and vastly exceeds the likes of the Manhattan 
Project and Apollo programme.

### Page 17

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 17

It is uncertain whether there will be enough demand to pay upfront for the 
investment in achieving RSI that would justify that investment. And whether the 
friction of verification, and inevitable declining marginal returns, act as a drag on 
what otherwise might be a runaway process. 
4. What happens if we don’t get there? The base case 
4.1 Separating AI progress from superintelligence 
So what if that thesis is wrong? Does the AI trade depend on a journey to AGI?  
AI can remain economically important even before RSI coupled with scaling laws 
lead to super intelligence . Companies do not need to wait for nebulous 
superintelligence to generate identifiable returns from AI in the medium term as 
they fold it into their systems, streamline workflows and roll out new business 
models. 
Investors already have exposure to RSI as a capital allocation because the leading 
US labs say RSI is a key part of their product strategy. And it is these labs whose 
products are catalysing and amplifying the infrastructure boom. 
Even r apid competitive diffusion does not mean every part of the physical 
economy changes instantly. 
The smartest superintelligence might identify the optimal buyout structure 
before breakfast, but it cannot force a financially constrained buyer to use it or 
persuade the seller to accept less than they have been expecting. Those 
challenges will be even more intractable where the parties are more complex, say, 
fiscally constrained sovereigns or foreign equity holders. 
4.2 Why the base case is attractive 
Superintelligence would be a chaotic accelerant to human society, and a 
potential regime break. But it is not the only scenario in which today’s investment 
in AI infrastructure makes economic sense. 
Adoption of AI tools by businesses is progressing approximately three times faster 
than the mobile, internet and cloud computing waves. From sector revenues 
below $1bn in 2022, the AI economy, excluding China, will likely exceed $200bn 
in revenues in 2026.  
Early winners are the cloud businesses. Google Cloud revenue jumped 82 percent 
from a year earlier in the most recent quarter as companies turned AI pilots into 
workflows. Amazon Web Services sales were up 37 percent and Microsoft’s AI 
business more than doubled. 
There is also slow, but increasing, evidence of a productivity uplift. The J-curve 
cuts both ways: front -loading investments is taking one step back to take two 
steps forward. The so -called complementary intangible investment that is 
(mis)measured as cost today to the detriment of the productivity statistics will 
flatter them in the years to come as it pays off. The base case does not require a 
regime break to show up as a productivity surprise, just for the accounting to 
catch up. 
The economic gains from AI that is less powerful than the AGI mooted by the labs 
would be substantial. And in this base case, they may also be attractive.

### Page 18

12 October 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 18

Hyperscaler capex is driving earnings at beneficiaries ranging from 
semiconductor makers to construction companies and accounting for between 
one-third and one -half of current US growth. The wealth effect of rising stock 
prices, with  the ratio of  US equity wealth to income rising from around 1.8 to 
almost 2.5 over the past three years, is supporting consumer spending. 
AI FOMO (fear of missing out) is buoying enterprise spending on cloud services 
and the groundwork for AI initiatives. Those are beginning to bear fruit in terms of 
personal productivity, cost savings and more targeted products and services. 
4.3 Investor watchpoints 
The arrival of ASI would be a disruptive economic shock. Investors need to be alert 
for the early signals. 
Rather than relying on a single probability forecast, investors should watch five 
indicators particularly closely for signs that AGI is nearing.  
1. AI speeds up AI 
• Track METR’s estimate of how much AI speeds up AI. Currently the 
capabilities of models are doubling roughly every three months. In a 
world of RSI, the pace of this speed-up will itself likely accelerate. 
2. AI can complete whole jobs 
• Track via the Remote Labor Index, a multi-sector benchmark. Currently it 
finds that the highest performing agent still only manages to automate 
20 percent of the real-world, economically valuable freelance projects it 
attempts. 
3. Compute remains scarce 
• On a path to superintelligence, demand for compute will continue to 
outrun supply. Track GPU one-year rental prices, up by about a third over 
the past year. 
4. Firms bifurcate 
• Leaders and laggards in the same industry diverge while averages stay 
the same. Look at revenue per employee in top - and bottom -quartile 
firms 
5. Junior hiring comes under significant pressure 
• … And then the pressure rises up the ranks.

报道称， Anthropic 本周已安排与关键投资者会⾯，⽬标是让其股票在下周四或周五开始交易。

该公司仍有可能推迟估值⽬标并等待更有利的市场条件，但这可能会引发市场恐慌。
此次 IPO 的进展与最终定价不仅关乎 Anthropic ⾃⾝的融资规模，更将成为整个 AI 产业链估值的
⻛向标，直接影响市场对 AI 基础设施⾼昂资本开⽀及其商业化前景的重新评估。
冲刺感恩节前上市，估值预期⾯临下调
Anthropic 此次 IPO 的时间表已进⼊关键阶段。
据彭博社报道，根据相关规定，在 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇公开提交招股书后， Anthropic 最快可于 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇开
始正式 IPO 营销。若进展顺利，公司有望在 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇当周完成定价，赶在 11 ⽉ 26 ⽇美国感恩节
假期前登陆纳斯达克，股票代码为 “ANT” 。
然⽽，市场的剧烈波动使得估值成为各⽅博弈的核⼼。数⽉前，市场曾讨论过约 2 万亿美元的估
值数字，但 Anthropic 现在⼏乎肯定需要接受⼀个低得多的估值。据彭博 6 ⽇援引⼀位知情⼈⼠
透露，部分潜在投资者讨论的估值已降⾄约 1.2 万亿⾄ 1.3 万亿美元，甚⾄有⼈考虑低⾄ 1.1 万亿

美元。
对于投⾏⽽⾔，定价策略⾄关重要。如果⽬标设定过⾼，投资者需求可能受损并导致股价下
跌；若区间过低，公司和银⾏家将⾯临 “ 把钱留在桌⾯上 ” 的批评。在实际操作中，银⾏家有时会
⿎励公司提出略低于预期的价格区间，以便随后以投资者需求强劲为由上调价格。预计本周提
交的⽂件将详细说明计划出售的股票数量及价格，并可能披露已锁定按 IPO 价格认购的知名投资
者。
政治与市场双重阻⼒， AI 概念⾯临重估
Anthropic 此次 IPO ⾯临的挑战不仅是投资者对其⾃⾝估值的分歧，更受到整个 AI 产业链⻛险偏
好变化的影响。美国中期选举后，⼀批对 AI 持怀疑态度的⼈⼠掌权，并出台了可能减缓美国数据
中⼼发展的暂停令，导致纳斯达克综合指数成分股在⼀⽇内蒸发了 2.5 万亿美元市值。

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
521 收藏 分享到 :
市场波动曾推动芝加哥期权交易所的波动率指数（ VIX ）升破 25 ⾄ 28 上⽅。 Renaissance
Capital ⾼级策略师 Matt Kennedy 表⽰，当 VIX 处于 25 以上⽔平时，企业通常会考虑推迟 IPO 。此
外，投资者对巨额 AI 投资及其潜在回报缺乏清晰度的担忧⽇益增加，此前 Anthropic 的竞争对⼿
OpenAI 在最新⼀轮融资中筹集的资⾦不到最初⽬标的⼀半，估值也低于⽬标的 1.25 万亿美元。
不过，市场随后出现了⼀定程度的修复。标普 500 指数和费城半导体指数的反弹，以及 VIX 指数
的回落，可能为 Anthropic 继续推进上市提供了额外的信⼼。 Matt Kennedy 认为， Anthropic 的
IPO 不同于⼀般企业上市，当前市场环境未必⾜以迫使其推迟上市，但投资者对折价和估值的要

求可能更⾼。
业绩⾼增⻓遭遇放缓，投资者紧盯算⼒成本
估值争议的另⼀核⼼，是 Anthropic 能否证明⾃⾝的营收增⻓⾜以⽀撑庞⼤的上市估值。招股书
显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 1127% 。但增⻓速度已出现放缓迹
象，第三季度环⽐增幅降⾄ 47% ，远低于第⼆季度 144% 的环⽐增速。
利润表现同样承压。第三季度， Anthropic 经营亏损 5.67 亿美元，净亏损达到 232.91 亿美元。业
绩转亏主要受到约 50 亿美元短期算⼒采购成本，以及约 20 亿美元员⼯捐赠匹配计划相关⾮现⾦
费⽤的影响。更令市场关注的是，公司披露的计算能⼒采购及基础设施承诺⽀出总额⾼达 8954
亿美元。
据路透社报道，投资者⽬前仍在等待 Anthropic 的 10 ⽉份营收数据，希望借此判断公司能否扭转
近期增⻓放缓的趋势。营收表现、企业客⼾使⽤量以及算⼒成本控制能⼒，将是路演期间投资
者重点追问的核⼼指标。
融资规模或创纪录，⾏业⻛向标意义凸显

作为⾸批以开发前沿 AI 模型为核⼼业务并尝试登陆公开市场的公司之⼀， Anthropic 此次发⾏的
规模和定价将为整个 AI ⾏业提供重要参照。
市场正密切关注 Anthropic 的 IPO 能否与 Elon Musk 旗下 SpaceX 创纪录的上市表现相媲美。
SpaceX 在此前的发⾏中估值约为 1.77 万亿美元，筹集资⾦规模在 750 亿美元⾄ 862 亿美元之间。
⽽据路透社报道， Anthropic 此次发⾏规模甚⾄可能达到 1000 亿美元。
由⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利牵头，另有 20 家银⾏参与的这笔超⼤型 IPO ，其实际认购情况将给出明确的
信号：在经历 AI 概念股⼤幅调整后，公开市场究竟愿意为前沿 AI 公司的⻓期增⻓潜⼒⽀付多⾼的
溢价。

Document 328: Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud

Source PDF: all report pdf/Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

私募巨头 Apollo 正以 750 亿美元的惊⼈押注重塑 AI 芯⽚融资格局 ——350 亿美元购买⾕歌
TPU 租赁给 Anthropic ， 400 亿美元购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚租赁给 SpaceX 。这⼀规模
远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave 的 270 亿美元，使 Apollo 跻⾝仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌的
全球芯⽚储备第四极。
摘要：
私募巨头 Apollo Global Management 正以前所未有的规模押注 AI 芯⽚市场，其两笔待完成的芯
⽚交易合计⾦额⾼达 750 亿美元，不仅远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来累计 270 亿
美元的芯⽚融资规模，更是排名第⼆的竞争对⼿的逾 10 倍。
据 The Information 最新报道，SpaceX 宣布将租⽤价值 400 亿美元的英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，⽽
Apollo 将通过旗下⼦公司购⼊这批芯⽚后再租赁给 SpaceX 。此前， Apollo 已于今年 6 ⽉宣布联

合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，为其购买⾕歌张量处理器（ TPU ）并租赁给 Anthropic 的交易安排了
350 亿美元融资。
⼀旦上述交易完成，在 AI 芯⽚持有规模上，Apollo 将仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌等超⼤规模云
服务商，成为全球芯⽚储备最雄厚的⾮超⼤规模玩家，彻底改变 AI 基础设施的竞争格局。 Apollo
在这些交易中通过撮合交易及管理融资基⾦赚取费⽤。
两笔巨型交易奠定芯⽚霸主地位
Apollo 当前的芯⽚布局由两笔核⼼交易构成。
其⼀是今年 6 ⽉宣布的 350 亿美元交易：Apollo 联合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，购买⾕歌 TPU 并租
赁给 AI 初创公司 Anthropic 。其⼆是上周随 SpaceX 公告浮出⽔⾯的 400 亿美元交易：Apollo 通过
⼦公司购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，再租赁给⻢斯克旗下的 SpaceX 。
两笔交易合计 750 亿美元，使 Apollo 在芯⽚融资领域的体量远超任何⼀家 neocloud 。据 The

Information 汇编的数据， CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来通过芯⽚抵押融资累计筹资 270 亿美元，位
居 neocloud 之⾸；其后依次为 Valor （ 54 亿美元）、 Nscale （ 49 亿美元）、 IREN （ 39 亿美元）、
Lambda （ 15 亿美元）、 Crusoe （ 11 亿美元）、 Nebius （ 7.75 亿美元）及 Sharon AI （ 3.56 亿美
元）。
值得注意的是，上述统计仅涵盖以 GPU 和 TPU 为抵押的融资，不包括各家 neocloud 为运营融资
⽽发⾏的可转换债券及信贷额度。此外，上周另有⼀笔由花旗集团、美国银⾏与 Blackstone 联合
安排、规模达 630 亿美元的交易 —— ⽤于为 Anthropic 采购 Broadcom 芯⽚提供融资 —— 亦未纳⼊
上述⽐较，原因是各⽅未披露这批芯⽚的实际持有⽅。
芯⽚融资模式： SPV 结构的规模化复制
Apollo 所采⽤的芯⽚融资结构，脱胎于 CoreWeave 与 Blackstone 在 2023 年共同开创的特殊⽬的

载体（ SPV ）模式。在这⼀安排下， neocloud 与其⾦融合作⽅共同设⽴ SPV ，由 SPV 发⾏债务
以购买芯⽚，芯⽚终端⽤⼾⽀付的租⾦则⽤于偿还债务。
Apollo 的操作逻辑与此⼀脉相承，但规模更为庞⼤。与许多 neocloud 不同的是， Apollo 并未额外
投⼊资本建设⽤于存放芯⽚的数据中⼼，其商业模式更为聚焦 —— 通过购买芯⽚、出租给终端
⽤⼾，赚取交易撮合费⽤及基⾦管理费。

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
超⼤规模云服务商则⽆需借助 SPV 结构，可直接通过⾃由现⾦流、股权发⾏或传统公司债完成
芯⽚采购，这也是 Apollo 与超⼤规模云服务商之间的本质区别。
保险资本驱动 Apollo 转型
Apollo 的芯⽚豪赌，折射出这家⽼牌私募机构过去⼗年的深刻转型。 Apollo 由亿万富翁 Leon
Black 联合创⽴，成⽴已逾 36 年，早年以杠杆收购闻名，其中包括 2008 年斥资近 300 亿美元收购
赌场巨头哈拉斯娱乐。
过去⼗年间， Apollo ⼤规模吸纳来⾃保险公司的资本。保险资⾦偏好收益率⾼于投资级债券、
但信⽤评级仍维持投资级的资产类别，⽽芯⽚融资交易恰好契合这⼀需求。这⼀资本结构的转
变，为 Apollo 在 AI 基础设施领域的⼤规模布局提供了充裕的弹药。

芯⽚融资对 Apollo ⽽⾔是⼀个全新的业务⽅向，但其背后的逻辑与传统私募信贷如出⼀辙：以资
产为抵押，提供结构化融资，赚取稳定的利差收益。随着 AI 算⼒需求持续膨胀， Apollo 正将这⼀
逻辑推向新的量级。
